add_namespace = GOT_Story_Country
add_namespace = GOT_Story_News

##SEASON 1##
############


#A Terror in the Woods
country_event = {
	id = GOT_Story_Country.1
	title = GOT_Story_Country.1.t
	desc = GOT_Story_Country.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country1

	trigger = {
		tag = NWA
		date > 1936.1.1
		date < 1936.2.1
	}
	
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = GOT_Story_Country.1.a

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
		}

		add_stability = -0.05
		
		hidden_effect = {
			NOR = {
				country_event = {
					id = GOT_Story_Country.2
					days = 1
				}
			}
		}

		every_other_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
			}
			news_event = {
				id = GOT_Story_News.1
				hours = 6
			}
		}
		
	}
}

news_event = {
	id = GOT_Story_News.1
	title = GOT_Story_News.1.t
	desc = GOT_Story_News.1.d
	picture = GFX_news_event_GOT_Story_News1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = GOT_Story_News.1.a
	}
}

#The King's Arrival
country_event = {
	id = GOT_Story_Country.3
	title = GOT_Story_Country.3.t
	desc = GOT_Story_Country.3.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country3

	trigger = {
		tag = NOR
		date > 1936.1.1
		date < 1936.2.1
	}
	
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = GOT_Story_Country.3.a

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 75
		}

		add_stability = -0.05
		
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = CRO
			modifier = RI_visited_capital
		}
		
		remove_country_leader_role = {
			character = NOR_ned_stark
			ideology = NR_subideology
		}
		
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = NOR_robb_stark
			country_leader = {
				expire = "1965.1.1"
				ideology = NR_subideology
				traits = { 
					warrior_code 
					great_war_hero 
					inexperienced_imperialist 
					reluctant_veteran 
					headstrong 
				}
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		
		CRO = {
			country_event = {
				id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.1
				hours = 6
			}
		}
		
		#Bran's Fall From The Tower
		hidden_effect = {
			NOR = {
				country_event = {
					id = GOT_Story_Country.5
					days = 1
				}
			}
		}
		
		every_other_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
			}
			news_event = {
				id = GOT_Story_News.3
				hours = 6
			}
		}
		
	}
	
	option = {
		name = GOT_Story_Country.3.b

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
		}

		add_stability = 0.05
		
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = CRO
			modifier = RI_visited_capital
		}
		
		CRO = {
			country_event = {
				id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.2
				hours = 6
			}
		}
		
		#Bran's Fall From The Tower
		hidden_effect = {
			NOR = {
				country_event = {
					id = GOT_Story_Country.5
					days = 1
				}
			}
		}
		
		every_other_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
			}
			news_event = {
				id = GOT_Story_News.3
				hours = 6
			}
		}
		
	}
}

#A Marriage for Power
country_event = {
	id = GOT_Story_Country.4
	title = GOT_Story_Country.4.t
	desc = GOT_Story_Country.4.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country4

	trigger = {
		tag = TAR
		date > 1936.1.1
		date < 1936.2.1
		
	}
	
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = GOT_Story_Country.4.a
		
		trigger = {
			country_exists = DOT
			NOT = { has_war_with = DOT }
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 75
		}
		
		add_threat = 3
		
		DOT = {
			country_event = {
				id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.3
				hours = 6
			}
		}

		
	}
	
	option = {
		name = GOT_Story_Country.4.b

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
				country_exists = DOT
				NOT = { has_war_with = DOT }
			}
		}
		
		country_event = {
			id = GOT_Alt_Country.1
			days = 1
		}

		add_stability = 0.05
		
	}
}

news_event = {
	id = GOT_Story_News.4
	title = GOT_Story_News.4.t
	desc = GOT_Story_News.4.d
	picture = GFX_news_event_GOT_Story_News4

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = GOT_Story_News.4.a
	}
}

	title = GOT_Story_Country.17.t
	desc = GOT_Story_Country.17.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country17

		tag = CRO
		date > 1936.4.15
		date < 1936.5.15
		CRO = { 
			CRO_ned_stark = { 
				is_hired_as_advisor = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = GOT_Story_Country.17.a

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 85
		}

		add_political_power = 25
		
		set_global_flag = GOT_Lord_Stark_Investigation_Flag
		
		WES = {
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = NOR
				modifier = RI_hostile_foreign_policy
			}
		}

		every_other_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
			}
			news_event = {
				id = GOT_Story_News.17
				hours = 6
			}
		}
		
	}
	
	option = { 	
		name = GOT_Story_Country.17.b

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 15
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
		}

		add_political_power = -25
		
	}
}

country_event = {

# Event 22 – Jaime Attacks Ned in the Streets
country_event = {
	id = GOT_Story_Country.22
	title = GOT_Story_Country.22.t
	desc = GOT_Story_Country.22.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country22

	trigger = {
		tag = CRO
		date > 1936.5.20
		date < 1936.6.20
		CRO = { 
			CRO_ned_stark = { 
				is_hired_as_advisor = yes
			}
		}
		has_global_flag = GOT_Tyrion_Captured_Flag
	}
	
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = GOT_Story_Country.22.a

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 70
		}

		add_stability = -0.05
		add_war_support = -0.05
		add_threat = 10

		every_other_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
			}
			news_event = {
				id = GOT_Story_News.22
				hours = 6
			}
		}
		
	}
	
	option = {
		name = GOT_Story_Country.22.b

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
		}
		
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				is_in_faction_with = WES
			}
		}

		add_stability = -0.05
		add_war_support = 0.05
		
		create_wargoal = {
			type = annex_everything
			target = WES
			expire = 365
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction = no
			}
			create_faction = "The Iron Throne"
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction = yes
			}
			NOR = {
				country_event = {
					id = generic.5
					hours = 6
				}
			}
		}

		every_other_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
			}
			news_event = {
				id = GOT_Story_News.22
				hours = 6
			}
		}
		
	}
}

	id = GOT_Story_News.22

	picture = GFX_news_event_GOT_Story_News22

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = GOT_Story_News.22.a
	}
}

#Robb Stark Is Declared King in the North
country_event = {
	id = GOT_Story_Country.47
	title = GOT_Story_Country.47.t
	desc = GOT_Story_Country.47.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country47

	trigger = {
		tag = NOR
		date > 1936.11.15
		date < 1936.12.15
		has_war_with = WES
		has_country_leader = {
			name = "Robb Stark"
			ruling_only = yes
		}
	}
	
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = GOT_Story_Country.47.a

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 60
		}

		add_stability = -0.05
		add_threat = 10
		
		set_global_flag = GOT_King_In_The_North_Flag
		
		RIV = {
			country_event = {
				id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.51
				hours = 6
			}
		}

		every_other_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
			}
			news_event = {
				id = GOT_Story_News.47
				hours = 6
			}
		}
		
	}
	
	# Option to bend the knee to Stannis if he's declared himself king
    option = {
        name = GOT_Story_Country.47.b
		
		ai_chance = {
            factor = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
        }

        trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = DRA
			}
			OR = {
				DRA = {
					has_country_flag = GOT_Claimed_Iron_Throne_Flag
				}
				CRO = {
					has_country_leader = {
						name = "Stannis Baratheon"
						ruling_only = yes
					}
				}
			}
        }

        add_stability = 0.10
        add_political_power = 50

        set_country_flag = GOT_Bent_The_Knee_Flag
		
		#If Stormlands is a faction leader, the faction is disbaned
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_faction_leader = yes
			}
			dismantle_faction = yes
		}

		#If Stormlands is a faction member, but not the leader, then he just leaves
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction = yes
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = DRA }
			}
			leave_faction = yes
			
		}
		
		#Dragonstone gets event asking them to join their faction
			every_other_country = {
				limit = {
					is_in_faction_with = DRA
					is_faction_leader = yes
				}
			   country_event = {
					id = generic.2
					hours = 6
				}
			}

        # Inform the world that bent the knee to Stannis
        news_event = {
			id = GOT_Diplomatic_News.11
			hours = 6
        }
    }
	
	# Option to bend the knee to Renly if he's declared himself king
    option = {
        name = GOT_Story_Country.47.c
		
		ai_chance = {
            factor = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
        }

        trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = STO
			}
			OR = {
				STO = {
					has_country_flag = GOT_Claimed_Iron_Throne_Flag
				}
				CRO = {
					has_country_leader = {
						name = "Renly Baratheon"
						ruling_only = yes
					}
				}
			}
        }

        add_stability = 0.10
        add_political_power = 50

        set_country_flag = GOT_Bent_The_Knee_Flag
		
		#If Dragonstone is a faction leader, the faction is disbaned
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_faction_leader = yes
			}
			dismantle_faction = yes
		}

		#If Dragonstone is a faction member, but not the leader, then he just leaves
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction = yes
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = STO }
			}
			leave_faction = yes
			
		}
		
		#Stormlands gets event asking them to join their faction
		every_other_country = {
				limit = {
					is_in_faction_with = STO
					is_faction_leader = yes
				}
			   country_event = {
					id = generic.2
					hours = 6
				}
			}

        # Inform the world that bent the knee to Renly
        news_event = {
			id = GOT_Diplomatic_News.12
			hours = 6
        }
    }
	
	option = {
		name = GOT_Story_Country.47.e

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
		}

		add_stability = 0.05
		
	}
}

		hidden_effect = {
			DOT = {
				country_event = {
					id = GOT_Story_Country.51
					days = 2
				}
			}
		}

		add_stability = -0.05
		add_threat = 3

		every_other_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
			}
			news_event = {
				id = GOT_Story_News.48
				hours = 6
			}
		}
		
	}
}

#King Robert Dies
country_event = {
	id = GOT_Story_Country.54
	title = GOT_Story_Country.54.t
	desc = GOT_Story_Country.54.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country54

	#Triggered by him getting wounded
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = GOT_Story_Country.54.a
		
		trigger = {
			CRO_ned_stark = { 
				is_hired_as_advisor = yes
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 75
		}

		add_stability = -0.05
		add_threat = 6
		
		kill_country_leader = yes
		
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = CRO_ned_stark
			country_leader = {
				expire = "1965.1.1"
				ideology = NR_subideology
				traits = { 
					warden_of_north 
					warrior_code 
					indomitable_perseverance 
					great_war_hero 
					strict_austerity_adherent 
					staunch_monarchist 
					fortification_engineer 
					reluctant_veteran 
				}
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		
		country_event = {
			id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.8
			days = 1
		}
		
		set_global_flag = GOT_King_Robert_Dead_Flag

		every_other_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
			}
			news_event = {
				id = GOT_Story_News.54
				hours = 6
			}
		}
		
		every_other_country = {
			limit = {
				has_guaranteed = CRO
			}
			diplomatic_relation = {
				country = CRO
				relation = guarantee
				active = no
			}
		}
		
		
		
	}
	
	option = {
		name = GOT_Story_Country.54.b

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
		}

		add_stability = -0.05
		add_threat = 6
		
		kill_country_leader = yes
		
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = CRO_cersei_lannister
			country_leader = {
				expire = "1965.1.1"
				ideology = NR_subideology
				traits = {  
					dictator  
					political_manipulator  
					autocratic_imperialist  
					#the_foreign_connection  
					#ambitious_negotiator_fascism  
					princess_of_terror  
				}
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					CRO_ned_stark = { 
						is_hired_as_advisor = yes
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_global_flag = GOT_Lord_Stark_Investigation_Flag
					}
					country_event = {
						id = GOT_Story_Country.36
						hours = 6
					}
				}
				else = {
					CRO_ned_stark = {
						retire = yes
					}
					NOR = {
						country_event = {
							id = GOT_Alt_Country.4
							hours = 6
						}
					}
					country_event = {
						id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.9
						days = 1
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		set_global_flag = GOT_King_Robert_Dead_Flag
		

		every_other_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
			}
			news_event = {
				id = GOT_Story_News.54
				hours = 6
			}
		}
		
		every_other_country = {
			limit = {
				has_guaranteed = CRO
			}
			diplomatic_relation = {
				country = CRO
				relation = guarantee
				active = no
			}
		}
		
	}
}

			#Khal Drogo Dies 100% for historical AI

				limit = {
					is_historical_focus_on = yes

				}

					id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.14
					days = 71
				}
			}
			#For Players
			else = {
				#25% He Lives, 75% He Dies
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						country_event = {
							id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.13
							days = 71
						}
					}
					75 = {
						country_event = {
							id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.14
							days = 71
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		every_other_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
			}
			news_event = {
				id = GOT_Story_News.55
				hours = 6
			}
		}
		
	}

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes

#2. Stannis Baratheon Declares His Claim to the Throne
country_event = {
    id = GOT_Story_Country.101
    title = GOT_Story_Country.101.t
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.101.d
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country101

    trigger = {
        tag = DRA
		date > 1937.1.5
        date < 1937.2.5
		country_exists = CRO
		CRO = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		is_subject = no
		#Obviously not Robert or Himself on the throne for him to make this decision
		NOT = {
			CRO = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Robert Baratheon"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			CRO = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Stannis Baratheon"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
		}
    }

    fire_only_once = yes

    option = {
        name = GOT_Story_Country.101.a

        ai_chance = {
            factor = 70
			 modifier = {
				factor = 1.35    # Boosts base 70 to 95 (70 * 1.35 = 94.5)
				has_global_flag = GOT_Lord_Stark_Investigation_Flag
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.71    # Reduces base 70 to ~50 (70 * 0.71 ≈ 50)
				NOT = { has_global_flag = GOT_Lord_Stark_Investigation_Flag }
			}
        }
		
		add_threat = 10
        add_stability = -0.05
        add_political_power = -50
        add_war_support = 0.05
		
		set_country_flag = GOT_Claimed_Iron_Throne_Flag
		
		#every_state = { 
		#	limit = { 
		#		is_core_of = CRO
		#	} 
		#	add_claim_by = DRA
		#}
		
		#If Dragonstone is in a faction with the Crownlands, then he leaves
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction_with = CRO
			}
			leave_faction = yes
		}
		
		create_wargoal = {
			type = annex_everything
			target = CRO
			expire = 365
		}
		
		#Davos Made Hand of The King
		add_advisor_role = {
			character = DRA_Davos_Seaworth
			activate = yes
			advisor = {
				slot = political_advisor
				idea_token = DRA_Davos_Seaworth
				can_be_fired = no
				traits = {
					hand_of_the_king
				}
			}
		}
		
		#Every other country informed of Stannis claim
        every_other_country = {
            limit = {
                exists = yes
            }
            news_event = {
                id = GOT_Story_News.101
                hours = 6
            }
        }
    }
	
	option = {
        name = GOT_Story_Country.101.b
		
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = CRO
			}
		}

        ai_chance = {
            factor = 25
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.2   # 25 * 0.2 = 5
				has_global_flag = GOT_Lord_Stark_Investigation_Flag
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2.0   # 25 * 2.0 = 50
				NOT = { has_global_flag = GOT_Lord_Stark_Investigation_Flag }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
        }

        add_stability = 0.05
        add_political_power = 25
		
		set_country_flag = GOT_Bent_The_Knee_Flag
		
		#If Dragonstone is a faction leader, the faction is disbaned
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_faction_leader = yes
			}
			dismantle_faction = yes
		}

		#If Dragonstone is a faction member, but not the leader, then he just leaves
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction = yes
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = CRO }
			}
			leave_faction = yes
			
		}
		
		#Crownlands gets event asking them to join their faction
			every_other_country = {
				limit = {
					is_in_faction_with = CRO
					is_faction_leader = yes
				}
			   country_event = {
					id = generic.2
					hours = 6
				}
			}
		
		#Everyone gets the generic Bend the Knee Event
        news_event = {
			id = GOT_Diplomatic_News.9
			hours = 6
        }
    }
	
	# Option to bend the knee to Renly if he's declared himself king
    option = {
        name = GOT_Story_Country.101.c
		
		ai_chance = {
            factor = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5   # 5 * 0.5 = 2.5 → investigation makes this even less likely
				has_global_flag = GOT_Lord_Stark_Investigation_Flag
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2.0   # 5 * 2.0 = 10 → without investigation, slightly more plausible
				NOT = { has_global_flag = GOT_Lord_Stark_Investigation_Flag }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
        }

        trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = STO
			}
			OR = {
				STO = {
					has_country_flag = GOT_Claimed_Iron_Throne_Flag
				}
				CRO = {
					has_country_leader = {
						name = "Renly Baratheon"
						ruling_only = yes
					}
				}
			}
        }

        add_stability = 0.10
        add_political_power = 50

        set_country_flag = GOT_Bent_The_Knee_Flag
		
		#If Dragonstone is a faction leader, the faction is disbaned
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_faction_leader = yes
			}
			dismantle_faction = yes
		}

		#If Dragonstone is a faction member, but not the leader, then he just leaves
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction = yes
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = STO }
			}
			leave_faction = yes
			
		}
		
		#Stormlands gets event asking them to join their faction
			every_other_country = {
				limit = {
					is_in_faction_with = STO
					is_faction_leader = yes
				}
			   country_event = {
					id = generic.2

    desc = GOT_Story_Country.102.d

    trigger = {
        tag = NOR
		date > 1937.1.5
        date < 1937.2.5
		has_war_with = CRO
		has_war_with = WES
		CRO = { is_in_faction_with = WES }
		has_global_flag = GOT_King_In_The_North_Flag
		surrender_progress < 0.5
		NOT = { has_global_flag = BPD_Disable_Them_Completely }
		has_country_leader = {
			name = "Robb Stark"
			ruling_only = yes
		}
    }

    fire_only_once = yes

            factor = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
        }

        add_war_support = -0.05
		
		every_other_country = {
            limit = {
                is_in_faction_with = CRO
				is_faction_leader = yes
            }
           country_event = {
                id = BPD_Better_Peace_Deals_Country.1
                hours = 6
            }
        }

        every_other_country = {
            limit = {
                exists = yes
            }
            news_event = {
                id = GOT_Story_News.102
                hours = 6
            }
        }
    }
	
	option = {
        name = GOT_Story_Country.102.b

        ai_chance = {
            factor = 60
        }
        add_war_support = 0.05
		
    }
}

# Melisandre's Shadow Birth
country_event = {
    id = GOT_Story_Country.119
    title = GOT_Story_Country.119.t
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.119.d
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country119

    trigger = {
        tag = DRA
        date > 1937.4.15
        date < 1937.5.15
		has_country_flag = GOT_Melisandre_Seduces_Stannis
		has_country_flag = GOT_Claimed_Iron_Throne_Flag
		STO = { has_country_flag = GOT_Claimed_Iron_Throne_Flag }
		NOT = { is_in_faction_with = STO }
    }

    fire_only_once = yes

    option = {
        name = GOT_Story_Country.119.a

        ai_chance = {
            factor = 60
        }
		
		#Renly Dies 100% for historical AI
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
				NOT = { has_global_flag = GOT_Sandbox_Mode }
			}
			STO = { 
				country_event = {
					id = GOT_Story_Country.120
					days = 14
				}
			}
		}
		#For Players, or Non Historical AI, Renly has a chance to survive
		else = {
		#50% Chance Renly Dies, 50% Renly Survives
			random_list = {
				#Renly Dies
				50 = {
					STO = { 
						country_event = {
							id = GOT_Story_Country.120
							days = 14
						}
					}
				}
				#Renly Survives
				50 = {
					STO = { 
						country_event = {
							id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.19
							days = 14
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

        add_stability = -0.05

        every_other_country = {

                exists = yes

            news_event = {
                id = GOT_Story_News.119
                hours = 6
            }
        }
    }

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}

# Renly Baratheon's Death
country_event = {
    id = GOT_Story_Country.120
    title = GOT_Story_Country.120.t
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.120.d
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country120

    is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
        name = GOT_Story_Country.120.a  # Swear fealty to Stannis
        ai_chance = {
            factor = 60
        }
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction_with = REA
			}
			REA = {
			   country_event = {
					id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.20
					hours = 6
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_war_with = DRA
			}
			DRA = {
			   country_event = {
					id = BPD_Better_Peace_Deals_Country.1
					hours = 1
				}
			}
		}
		
		kill_country_leader = yes
		
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = STO_Stannis_Baratheon
			country_leader = {
				expire = "1965.1.1"
				ideology = NR_subideology
				traits = { 
					legitimate_heir 
					warrior_code 
					unpopular_monarch 
					headstrong 
					indomitable_perseverance 
					strict_austerity_adherent 
					stout_defender 
					inexperienced_imperialist 
					autocratic_imperialist 
					cornered_fox 
				}
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}

        add_political_power = -50
		
		 set_country_flag = GOT_Bent_The_Knee_Flag
		
		#If Stormlands is a faction leader, the faction is disbaned
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_faction_leader = yes
			}
			dismantle_faction = yes
		}

		#If Stormlands is a faction member, but not the leader, then he just leaves
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction = yes
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = DRA }
			}
			leave_faction = yes
			
		}
		
		#Dragonstone gets event asking them to join their faction
			every_other_country = {
				limit = {
					is_in_faction_with = DRA
					is_faction_leader = yes
				}
			   country_event = {
					id = generic.2
					hours = 6
				}
			}

        # Inform the world that Renly bent the knee to Stannis
        news_event = {
			id = GOT_Diplomatic_News.11
			hours = 6
        }
		
		country_event = {
			id = GOT_Location_Country.4
			hours = 6
		}
		
		every_other_country = {
            limit = {
                exists = yes
            }
            news_event = {
                id = GOT_Story_News.120
                hours = 6
            }
        }
		
    }

    option = {
        name = GOT_Story_Country.120.b  # Swear loyalty to the Iron Throne
        ai_chance = {
            factor = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
        }
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction_with = REA
			}
			REA = {
			   country_event = {
					id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.20
					hours = 6
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_war_with = CRO
			}
			every_other_country = {
				limit = {
					is_in_faction_with = CRO
					is_faction_leader = yes
				}
			   country_event = {
					id = BPD_Better_Peace_Deals_Country.1
					hours = 1
				}
			}
		}

		kill_country_leader = yes


        if = {
            limit = { 
				CRO = {
					has_country_leader = {
						name = "Joffrey Baratheon"
						ruling_only = yes
					}
				}
			}
			remove_country_leader_role = {
				character = STO_Stannis_Baratheon
				ideology = FR_subideology
			}
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = STO_joffrey_baratheon
				country_leader = {
					expire = "1965.1.1"
					ideology = NR_subideology
					traits = { 
						warmonger 
						underage_monarch 
						unpopular_monarch 
						incompetent_leader 
						dictator 
					}
				}
				promote_leader = yes
			}
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = STO_joffrey_baratheon
				country_leader = {
					expire = "1965.1.1"
					ideology = FR_subideology
					traits = { 
						warmonger 
						underage_monarch 
						unpopular_monarch 
						incompetent_leader 
						dictator 
					}
				}
				promote_leader = yes
			}
        }
        else_if = {
			limit = { 
				CRO = {
					has_country_leader = {
						name = "Tommen Baratheon"
						ruling_only = yes
					}
				}
			}
			remove_country_leader_role = {
				character = STO_Shireen_Baratheon
				ideology = DR_subideology
			}
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = STO_Tommen_Baratheon
				country_leader = {
					expire = "1965.1.1"
					ideology = NR_subideology
					traits = { 
						underage_monarch 
						compassionate_gentleman 
						#humble 
						appeaser 
						constitutional_monarch 
						popular_figurehead 
						champion_of_peace_5 
						voice_of_restraint 
					}
				}
				promote_leader = yes
			}
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = STO_Tommen_Baratheon
				country_leader = {
					expire = "1965.1.1"
					ideology = DR_subideology
					traits = { 
						underage_monarch 
						compassionate_gentleman 
						#humble 
						appeaser 
						constitutional_monarch 
						popular_figurehead 
						champion_of_peace_5 
						voice_of_restraint 
					}
				}
				promote_leader = yes
			}
        }
        else_if = {
           limit = { 
				CRO = {
					has_country_leader = {
						name = "Myrcella Baratheon"
						ruling_only = yes
					}
				}
			}
			remove_country_leader_role = {
				character = STO_Davos_Seaworth
				ideology = CR_subideology
			}
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = STO_Myrcella_Baratheon
				country_leader = {
					expire = "1965.1.1"
					ideology = NR_subideology
					traits = { 
						popular_figurehead 
						constitutional_monarch 
					}
				}
				promote_leader = yes
			}
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = STO_Myrcella_Baratheon
				country_leader = {
					expire = "1965.1.1"
					ideology = CR_subideology
					traits = { 
						popular_figurehead 
						constitutional_monarch 
					}
				}
				promote_leader = yes
			}
        }
        else = {
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = STO_Cortnay_Penrose
				country_leader = {
					expire = "1965.1.1"
					ideology = NR_subideology
					traits = { 
						stout_defender
						indomitable_perseverance
						hardened_officer
						conservative_grandee
					}
				}
				promote_leader = yes
			}
        }

        add_political_power = -25
        add_stability = -0.05
		
		set_country_flag = GOT_Bent_The_Knee_Flag
		
		#If Stormlands is a faction leader, the faction is disbaned
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_faction_leader = yes
			}
			dismantle_faction = yes
		}

		#If Stormlands is a faction member, but not the leader, then he just leaves
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction = yes
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = CRO }
			}
			leave_faction = yes
			
		}
		
		#Crownlands gets event asking them to join their faction
			every_other_country = {
				limit = {
					is_in_faction_with = CRO
					is_faction_leader = yes
				}
			   country_event = {
					id = generic.2
					hours = 6
				}
			}
		
		#Everyone gets the generic Bend the Knee Event
        news_event = {
			id = GOT_Diplomatic_News.9
			hours = 6
        }
		
		country_event = {
			id = GOT_Location_Country.4
			hours = 6
		}
		
		every_other_country = {
            limit = {
                exists = yes
            }
            news_event = {
                id = GOT_Story_News.120
                hours = 6
            }
        }
		
    }

    option = {
        name = GOT_Story_Country.120.c  # Let a castellan rule independently
        ai_chance = {
            factor = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
        }
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction_with = REA
			}
			REA = {
			   country_event = {
					id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.20
					hours = 6
				}
			}
		}
		
		kill_country_leader = yes
		
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = STO_Cortnay_Penrose
			country_leader = {
				expire = "1965.1.1"
				ideology = NR_subideology
				traits = { 
					stout_defender
					indomitable_perseverance
					hardened_officer
					conservative_grandee
				}
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}

        add_stability = 0.05
        add_war_support = -0.05
		
		every_other_country = {
            limit = {
                exists = yes
            }
            news_event = {
                id = GOT_Story_News.120
                hours = 6
            }
        }
		
    }

country_event = {

    title = GOT_Story_Country.121.t
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.121.d
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country121

    trigger = {
        tag = NOR
        date > 1937.5.20
        date < 1938.5.20
		has_war_with = CRO
		is_in_faction_with = RIV
		NOT = {
			has_war_with = STO
		}
		NOT = {
			is_in_faction_with = STO
		}
		NOT = {
			STO = {
				is_in_faction_with = CRO
			}
		}
		country_exists = STO
		STO = {
			has_country_leader = {
				name = "Renly Baratheon"
				ruling_only = yes
			}
		}
		has_country_leader = {
			name = "Robb Stark"
			ruling_only = yes
		}
		NOT = { has_country_flag = GOT_Bent_The_Knee_Flag }
    }

    fire_only_once = yes

    option = {
        name = GOT_Story_Country.121.a

        ai_chance = {
            factor = 65
        }

        add_stability = -0.05
		add_political_power = 25

        every_other_country = {
            limit = {
                exists = yes
            }
            news_event = {
                id = GOT_Story_News.121
                hours = 6
            }
        }
    }
	
	option = {
        name = GOT_Story_Country.121.b

        ai_chance = {
            factor = 35
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
        }

        add_stability = 0.05
		add_political_power = -25

    }
}

#Renly Baratheon Declares His Claim to the Throne
country_event = {
    id = GOT_Story_Country.150
    title = GOT_Story_Country.150.t
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.150.d
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country150

    trigger = {
        tag = STO
		date > 1937.1.5
        date < 1937.2.5
		country_exists = CRO
		OR = {
			CRO = {
				is_subject = no
			}
			CRO = {
				is_subject_of = DRA
			}
		}
		is_subject = no
		#Obviously not Robert or Himself on the throne for him to make this decision
		NOT = {
			CRO = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Robert Baratheon"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			CRO = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Renly Baratheon"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
		}
    }

    fire_only_once = yes

    option = {
        name = GOT_Story_Country.150.a

        ai_chance = {
            factor = 70
			 modifier = {
				factor = 1.35
				has_global_flag = GOT_Lord_Stark_Investigation_Flag
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.6
				NOT = { has_global_flag = GOT_Lord_Stark_Investigation_Flag }
			}
        }

		add_threat = 10
        add_stability = -0.05
        add_political_power = -50
        add_war_support = 0.05
		
		set_country_flag = GOT_Claimed_Iron_Throne_Flag
		
		#every_state = { 
		#	limit = { 
		#		is_core_of = CRO
		#	} 
		#	add_claim_by = STO
		#}
		
		#If Dragonstone is in a faction with the Crownlands, then he leaves
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction_with = CRO
			}
			leave_faction = yes
		}
		
		#Tournament or War Event
		country_event = {
                id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.15
                hours = 6
        }

		#Every other country informed of Renly claim
        every_other_country = {
            limit = {
                exists = yes
            }
            news_event = {
                id = GOT_Story_News.150
                hours = 6
            }
        }
    }
	
	option = {
        name = GOT_Story_Country.150.b
		
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = CRO
			}
		}

        ai_chance = {
            factor = 15
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				has_global_flag = GOT_Lord_Stark_Investigation_Flag
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.4
				NOT = { has_global_flag = GOT_Lord_Stark_Investigation_Flag }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
        }

        add_stability = 0.05
        add_political_power = 25
		
		set_country_flag = GOT_Bent_The_Knee_Flag
		
		#If Stormlands is a faction leader, the faction is disbaned
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_faction_leader = yes
			}
			dismantle_faction = yes
		}

		#If Stormlands is a faction member, but not the leader, then he just leaves
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction = yes
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = CRO }
			}
			leave_faction = yes
			
		}
		
		#Crownlands gets event asking them to join their faction
			every_other_country = {
				limit = {
					is_in_faction_with = CRO
					is_faction_leader = yes
				}
			   country_event = {
					id = generic.2
					hours = 6
				}
			}
		
		#Everyone gets the generic Bend the Knee Event
        news_event = {
			id = GOT_Diplomatic_News.9
			hours = 6
        }
    }
	
	 # Option to bend the knee to Stannis if he's declared himself king
    option = {
        name = GOT_Story_Country.150.c
		
		ai_chance = {
            factor = 15
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.1
				has_global_flag = GOT_Lord_Stark_Investigation_Flag
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.2
				NOT = { has_global_flag = GOT_Lord_Stark_Investigation_Flag }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
        }

        trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = DRA
			}
			OR = {
				DRA = {
					has_country_flag = GOT_Claimed_Iron_Throne_Flag
				}
				CRO = {
					has_country_leader = {
						name = "Stannis Baratheon"
						ruling_only = yes
					}
				}
			}
        }

        add_stability = 0.10
        add_political_power = 50

        set_country_flag = GOT_Bent_The_Knee_Flag
		
		#If Stormlands is a faction leader, the faction is disbaned
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_faction_leader = yes
			}
			dismantle_faction = yes
		}

		#If Stormlands is a faction member, but not the leader, then he just leaves
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction = yes
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = DRA }
			}
			leave_faction = yes
			
		}
		
		#Dragonstone gets event asking them to join their faction
			every_other_country = {
				limit = {
					is_in_faction_with = DRA
					is_faction_leader = yes
				}
			   country_event = {
					id = generic.2
					hours = 6
				}
			}

        # Inform the world that Renly bent the knee to Stannis
        news_event = {
			id = GOT_Diplomatic_News.11
			hours = 6
        }
    }
}

# Tyrion Becomes Master of Coin
country_event = { 
    id = GOT_Story_Country.212 
    title = GOT_Story_Country.212.t 
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.212.d 
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country212 
 
    trigger = { 
        tag = CRO
		date > 1938.3.10
        date < 1938.4.10		
		GOT_Lannister_Is_King = yes
		NOT = {
			WES = { 
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Tyrion Lannister"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
		}
    } 
 
    fire_only_once = yes 
 
    option = { 
        name = GOT_Story_Country.212.a 
 
        ai_chance = { 
            factor = 10 
        } 
 
        add_stability = 0.05 
        add_political_power = 25 
		
		remove_advisor_role = {
			character = "CRO_Tyrion_Lannister"
			slot = political_advisor
		}
		
		add_advisor_role = {
			character = CRO_tywin_lannister
			activate = yes
			advisor = {
				slot = political_advisor
				idea_token = CRO_tywin_lannister
				can_be_fired = no
				traits = {
					hand_of_the_king
				}
			}
		}
		
		add_advisor_role = {
			character = CRO_Tyrion_Lannister
			activate = yes
			advisor = {
				slot = political_advisor
				idea_token = CRO_Tyrion_Lannister

 # Robb Stark Executes Lord Karstark, Losing Key Allies
country_event = {
    id = GOT_Story_Country.223
    title = GOT_Story_Country.223.t
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.223.d
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country223

    trigger = {
        tag = NOR
        date > 1938.5.20
        date < 1938.6.20
        has_country_leader = {
            name = "Robb Stark"
            ruling_only = yes
        }
        has_war_with = WES
		OR = {
			AND = {
				NOT = { country_exists = KAR }
				NOR = { owns_state = 154 }
			}
			NOT = { KAR = { is_puppet_of = NOR } }
		}
    }

    fire_only_once = yes

    option = {
        name = GOT_Story_Country.223.a  # Execute Lord Karstark
        ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
		
		set_country_flag = GOT_Karhold_Released
		set_country_flag = GOT_Lord_Karstark_Executed

        add_stability = -0.05
        add_political_power = -25
        add_war_support = -0.05
		add_threat = 4
		
		release = KAR
		end_puppet = KAR
		
		
		
		KAR = {
			load_oob = KAR_1936
			if  = {
				limit = {
					has_country_leader = {
						name = "Rickard Karstark"
						ruling_only = yes
					}
				}
				kill_country_leader = yes
				add_country_leader_role = {
					character = KAR_Harald_Karstark
					country_leader = {
						expire = "1965.1.1"
						ideology = NR_subideology
						traits = {
							legitimate_heir
							inexperienced_imperialist
							autocratic_imperialist
							collaborator_king
							sins_of_the_father
						}
					}
					promote_leader = yes
				}
			}
            leave_faction = yes
			if  = {
				limit = {
					has_war_with = CRO
				}
				every_country = {
					limit = { is_in_faction_with = CRO }
					white_peace = KAR
					set_truce = { target = KAR days = 365 }
				}
				KAR = {
					white_peace = CRO
					set_truce = { target = CRO days = 365 }
				}
			}
            add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = NOR
				modifier = RI_super_hostile_foreign_policy
			}
        }
		
		every_state = { 
			limit = { 
				is_core_of = KAR
			} 
			add_core_of = NOR
		}

        every_other_country = {
            limit = { exists = yes }
            news_event = { id = GOT_Story_News.223 hours = 6 }
        }
    }

    option = {
        name = GOT_Story_Country.223.b  # Hold Lord Karstark hostage
        ai_chance = { 
			factor = 30 
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
		}
		
		set_country_flag = GOT_Karhold_Released

        add_political_power = -15
		
		release = KAR
		puppet = KAR
		
		KAR = {
			load_oob = KAR_1936
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = NOR
				modifier = RI_hostile_foreign_policy
			}
        }
		
		every_state = { 
			limit = { 
				is_core_of = KAR
			} 
			add_core_of = NOR
		}

        every_other_country = {
            limit = { exists = yes }
            news_event = { id = GOT_Diplomatic_News.17 hours = 6 }
        }
    }

    option = {
        name = GOT_Story_Country.223.c  # Pardon Lord Karstark
        ai_chance = { 
			factor = 30 
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
		}

        add_political_power = -40
        add_stability = 0.02

        every_other_country = {
            limit = { exists = yes }
            news_event = { id = GOT_Diplomatic_News.18 hours = 6 }
        }
    }
}

# Robb Stark Seeks Alliance with House Frey

    id = GOT_Story_Country.229 
    title = GOT_Story_Country.229.t 
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.229.d 
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country229 
 
    trigger = { 
        tag = NOR
		date > 1938.6.25
        date < 1938.7.25
		is_in_faction_with = RIV
		has_war_with = WES
		has_country_leader = {
			name = "Robb Stark"
			ruling_only = yes
		}
		RIV = {
			has_country_leader = {
				name = "Edmure Tully"
				ruling_only = yes
			}
		}
		has_global_flag = GOT_Robb_Marries_Talissa_Flag
		has_global_flag = GOT_NOR_FRE_Promise_Flag
    } 
 
    fire_only_once = yes 
 
    option = { 
        name = GOT_Story_Country.229.a 
 
        ai_chance = { 
            factor = 80 
        } 
		
		set_global_flag = GOT_Edmure_To_Marry_Roslin_Flag
 
        add_stability = 0.05 
        add_war_support = 0.05 
        add_political_power = 25 
		add_threat = 2
 
        every_other_country = { 
            limit = { 
                exists = yes 
            } 
            news_event = { 
                id = GOT_Story_News.229 
                hours = 6 
            } 
        } 
    } 
	
	option = { 
        name = GOT_Story_Country.229.b 
 
        ai_chance = { 
            factor = 20 
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
        } 
		
		set_global_flag = GOT_Freys_Insulted_Flag
 
        add_stability = -0.05 
        add_war_support = -0.05 
        add_political_power = -25 
  
    }
} 
 
# Robb Stark Seeks Alliance with House Frey
news_event = { 
    id = GOT_Story_News.229

    picture = GFX_news_event_GOT_Story_News229 
 
    is_triggered_only = yes 
 
    option = { 
        name = GOT_Story_News.229.a 
    } 
}

# Daenerys Confronts Yunkai's Emissary
country_event = { 
    id = GOT_Story_Country.232 
    title = GOT_Story_Country.232.t 
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.232.d 
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country232 
 
    trigger = { 
        tag = YUN
		date > 1938.8.1
        date < 1939.8.1
		TAR = { controls_state = 228 }
		NOT = { has_war_with = TAR } 
    } 
 
    fire_only_once = yes 
 
    option = { 
        name = GOT_Story_Country.232.a 
 
        ai_chance = { 
            factor = 60 
        } 
		
		TAR = { 
            country_event = { 
                id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.28
                hours = 6 
            } 
        }
 
        add_war_support = -0.05 
		add_threat = 4
    } 
	
	option = { 
        name = GOT_Story_Country.232.b 
 
        ai_chance = { 
            factor = 40 
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
        } 
		
        add_war_support = 0.05 
    } 
}

# Daenerys Gains a New Ally in Daario Naharis
country_event = { 
    id = GOT_Story_Country.238 
    title = GOT_Story_Country.238.t 
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.238.d 
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country238 
	
	hidden = yes
 
    trigger = { 
        tag = TAR
		date > 1938.9.5
        date < 1939.9.5
		228 = { is_controlled_by_ROOT_or_subject = yes }
    } 
 
    fire_only_once = yes 
 
    option = { 
        name = GOT_Story_Country.238.a 
 
        ai_chance = { 
            factor = 10 
        } 
		
		add_threat = 4
		
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_historical_focus_on = yes
					NOT = { has_global_flag = GOT_Sandbox_Mode }
				}
				TAR= {
					country_event = {
						id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.40
						hours = 6
					}
				}
			}
			#For Non-Historical/Sandbox 70-30
			else = {
				random_list = {
					#70% Defects Normally
					70 = {
						TAR = {
							country_event = {
								id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.40
								hours = 6
							}
						}
					}
					#30% Stays Loyal to Yunkai
					30 = {
						YUN = {
							country_event = {
								id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.41
								hours = 6
							}
						}	
					}
				}
			}
 
        
    } 
}

# The Red Wedding
country_event = { 
    id = GOT_Story_Country.241 
    title = GOT_Story_Country.241.t 
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.241.d 
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country241 
	
	hidden = yes
 
    trigger = { 
        tag = NOR
		date > 1938.10.10
        date < 1939.10.10
		is_in_faction_with = RIV
		has_country_leader = {
			name = "Robb Stark"
			ruling_only = yes
		}
		has_war_with = WES
		WES = {
			has_capitulated = no
		}
    } 
 
    fire_only_once = yes 
 
    option = { 
        name = GOT_Story_Country.241.a 
 
        ai_chance = { 
            factor = 10 
        } 
		
		#If Robb Doesn't Cross at the Twins
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = GOT_NOR_FRE_Promise_Flag }
			}
			random_list = {
				#35% Chance of a Great Betrayal
				35 = {
					country_event = {
						id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.34
						hours = 6
					}	
				}
				#65% Chance of Nothing Happening
				65 = {
					
				}
			}
		}
		#If Robb Crosses and then Honors his Wedding to Roslin
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = GOT_NOR_FRE_Promise_Flag
				has_global_flag = GOT_Robb_Honors_Marriage_Flag
			}
			random_list = {
				#85% Wedding Happens Normally
				85 = {
					country_event = {
						id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.36
						hours = 6
					}
				}
				#15% Red Wedding Cause Walder Frey is a Sneaky Bastard
				15 = {
					country_event = {
						id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.37
						hours = 6
					}	
				}
			}
		}
		#If Robb Crosses and then Breaks his Vows, but promises Edmure to Roslin
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = GOT_NOR_FRE_Promise_Flag
				has_global_flag = GOT_Robb_Marries_Talissa_Flag
				has_global_flag = GOT_Edmure_To_Marry_Roslin_Flag
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_historical_focus_on = yes
					NOT = { has_global_flag = GOT_Sandbox_Mode }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.39
					hours = 6
				}
			}
			#For Non-Historical/Sandbox 50-50
			else = {
				random_list = {
					#50% Wedding Happens Normally
					50 = {
						country_event = {
							id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.38
							hours = 6
						}
					}
					#50% Red Wedding Cause Walder Frey is a Sneaky Bastard
					50 = {
						country_event = {
							id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.39
							hours = 6
						}	
					}
				}
			}
		}
		#If Robb Crosses and then Breaks his Vows, and spurns the freys
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = GOT_NOR_FRE_Promise_Flag
				has_global_flag = GOT_Robb_Marries_Talissa_Flag
				has_global_flag = GOT_Freys_Insulted_Flag
			}
			country_event = {
				id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.35
				hours = 6
			}	
		}
 
    } 
}

        } 
    }

# Daenerys Plans the Invasion of Yunkai
country_event = { 
    id = GOT_Story_Country.244 
    title = GOT_Story_Country.244.t 
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.244.d 
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country244 
 
    trigger = { 
        tag = TAR
		date > 1938.10.10
        date < 1939.10.10
		country_exists = YUN
		228 = { is_controlled_by_ROOT_or_subject = yes }
		NOT = { has_non_aggression_pact_with = YUN }
    } 
 
    fire_only_once = yes 
 
    option = { 
        name = GOT_Story_Country.244.a 
 
        ai_chance = { 
            factor = 10 
        } 
 
        add_war_support = 0.05 
		add_threat = 3
		
		add_state_core = 33
		add_state_core = 91
		
		create_wargoal = {
			type = annex_everything
			target = YUN
			expire = 365
		}
 
        every_other_country = { 
            limit = { 
                exists = yes 
            } 
            news_event = { 
                id = GOT_Story_News.244 
                hours = 6 
            } 
        } 
    } 
}

# Death of Hoster Tully
country_event = { 
    id = GOT_Story_Country.251 
    title = GOT_Story_Country.251.t 
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.251.d 
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country251 
 
    trigger = { 
        tag = RIV
		date > 1938.3.10
        date < 1938.4.10
		has_country_leader = {
			name = "Hoster Tully"
			ruling_only = yes
		}
    } 
 
    fire_only_once = yes 
 
    option = { 
        name = GOT_Story_Country.251.a 
 
        ai_chance = { 
            factor = 10 
        } 
 
        add_stability = -0.05 
		add_threat = 1
		
		kill_country_leader = yes
		
		add_country_leader_role = {
				character = RIV_Edmure_Tully
				country_leader = {
					expire = "1965.1.1"
					ideology = NR_subideology
					traits = {  
						incompetent_leader  
						headstrong  
						stout_defender  
						reluctant_veteran  
						unpopular_monarch  
					}
				}
				promote_leader = yes
			}
		
		if = { 
            limit = { 
				NOR = {
					has_country_leader = {
						name = "Robb Stark"
						ruling_only = yes
					}
					NOT = { has_war_with = RIV }
				}
            } 
			NOR = {
				country_event = { 
					id = GOT_Story_Country.214  
					hours = 6 
				} 
			}
        }
		
		country_event = {
			id = GOT_Location_Country.8
			hours = 6
		}
 
        every_other_country = { 
            limit = { 
                exists = yes 
            } 
            news_event = { 
                id = GOT_Story_News.251 
                hours = 6 
            } 
        } 
    } 
}

			}

				expire = "1965.1.1"

				traits = {  
					dictator

					#the_foreign_connection

				}
			}

		}
 
        every_other_country = { 
            limit = { 
                exists = yes 
            } 
            news_event = { 
                id = GOT_Story_News.306 
                hours = 6 
            } 
        } 
    }

				character = VAL_Petyr_Baelish

					expire = "1965.1.1"
					ideology = NR_subideology
					traits = {  
						political_manipulator  
						ambitious_negotiator_neutrality  
						dealbroker

						backroom_backstabber  
					}
				}
				promote_leader = yes
			}
			
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = VAL_Petyr_Baelish
				country_leader = {
					expire = "1965.1.1"
					ideology = FR_subideology
					traits = {  
						political_manipulator  
						ambitious_negotiator_neutrality  
						dealbroker  
						smooth_talking_charmer  
						backroom_backstabber  
					}
				}
				promote_leader = yes
			}
 
        every_other_country = { 
            limit = { 
                exists = yes 
            } 
            news_event = { 
                id = GOT_Story_News.331 
                hours = 6 
            } 
        } 
    }

 # Oberyn Martell's Trial by Combat
country_event = { 
    id = GOT_Story_Country.336 
    title = GOT_Story_Country.336.t 
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.336.d 
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country336 
 
    trigger = { 
        tag = CRO 
		date > 1939.9.5
        date < 1940.9.5
		has_global_flag = GOT_Oberyn_Tyrions_Champion
    } 
 
    fire_only_once = yes 
 
    option = { 
        name = GOT_Story_Country.336.a 
 
        ai_chance = { 
            factor = 10 
        } 
		
		add_threat = 4
 
        #Oberyn Dies 100% for historical AI
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
				NOT = { has_global_flag = GOT_Sandbox_Mode }
			}
			DOR = { 
				country_event = {
					id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.31
					hours = 6
				}
			}
			set_global_flag = GOT_Oberyn_Dies
			every_other_country = { 
				limit = { 
					exists = yes 
				} 
				news_event = { 
					id = GOT_Story_News.336 
					hours = 6 
				} 
			} 
		}
		#For Non Historical AI, Oberyn has a chance to survive
		else = {
		#55% Chance Oberyn Dies, 45% Renly Survives
			random_list = {
				#Oberyn Dies
				55 = {
					DOR = { 
						country_event = {
							id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.31
							hours = 6
						}
					}
					set_global_flag = GOT_Oberyn_Dies
					every_other_country = { 
						limit = { 
							exists = yes 
						} 
						news_event = { 
							id = GOT_Story_News.336 
							hours = 6 
						} 
					}
				}
				#Oberyn Survives
				45 = {
					DOR = { 
						country_event = {
							id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.32
							hours = 6
						}
					}
					every_other_country = { 
						limit = { 
							exists = yes 
						} 
						news_event = { 
							id = GOT_Diplomatic_News.20
							hours = 6 
						} 
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		
    } 
}

    title = GOT_Story_Country.414.t 
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.414.d 
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country414 
 
    trigger = { 
        tag = CRO
		date > 1940.3.10
        date < 1940.4.10
		OR = {
			has_country_leader = {
				name = "Tommen Baratheon"
				ruling_only = yes
			}
			has_country_leader = {
				name = "Cersei Lannister"
				ruling_only = yes
			}
		}
    } 
 
    fire_only_once = yes 
 
    option = { 
        name = GOT_Story_Country.414.a 
 
        ai_chance = { 
            factor = 10 
        } 
 
		add_stability = 0.05
        add_political_power = 25 
		set_global_flag = GOT_Sparrows_Empowered_Flag
 
        every_other_country = { 
            limit = { 
                exists = yes 
            } 
            news_event = { 
                id = GOT_Story_News.414 
                hours = 6 
            } 
        } 
    } 
}

# 421 — Jon Snow Seeks Alliance with the Wildlings
country_event = { 
    id = GOT_Story_Country.421 
    title = GOT_Story_Country.421.t 
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.421.d 
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country421 
 
    trigger = { 
        tag = NWA
		date > 1940.5.20
        date < 1941.5.20
		country_exists = WIL
		country_exists = ICE
    } 
 
    fire_only_once = yes 
 
    option = { 
        name = GOT_Story_Country.421.a 
 
        ai_chance = { 
            factor = 65
        } 
 
        add_stability = -0.05 
		
		remove_ideas = neutrality_idea
		remove_ideas = RI_neutrality_idea
		
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction = no
			}
			create_faction = "The Pact of the Living"
		}
		
		WIL = {
			country_event = {
				id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.63
				hours = 6
			}
		}
 
       
    } 
	
	option = { 
        name = GOT_Story_Country.421.b
 
        ai_chance = { 
            factor = 35 
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
        } 
 
        add_stability = 0.05 
		
		WIL = {
			country_event = {
				id = GOT_Diplomatic_Country.66
				hours = 6
			}
		}
		
		
    } 
}

# 424 — Stannis Marches Toward Winterfell
country_event = { 
    id = GOT_Story_Country.424 
    title = GOT_Story_Country.424.t 
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.424.d 
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country424 
 
    trigger = { 
        tag = DRA
		date > 1940.5.20
        date < 1940.6.20
		has_country_leader = {
			name = "Stannis Baratheon"
			ruling_only = yes
		}
		country_exists = BOL
		BOL = { controls_state = 156 } 
		has_global_flag = GOT_Stannis_Aids_Wall_Flag
    } 
 
    fire_only_once = yes 
 
    option = { 
        name = GOT_Story_Country.424.a 
 
        ai_chance = { 
            factor = 65 
        } 
		
		add_war_support = 0.05
		
		declare_war_on = {
			target = BOL
			type = annex_everything
		}
		
		load_oob = DRA_Winterfell_Invasion
		
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = RI_major_operation
			days = 60
		}
 
        every_other_country = { 
            limit = { 
                exists = yes 
            } 
            news_event = { 
                id = GOT_Story_News.424 
                hours = 6 
            } 
        } 
    } 
	
	
	option = { 
        name = GOT_Story_Country.424.b
 
        ai_chance = { 
            factor = 35
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
        } 
		
		add_war_support = -0.05

    } 
}

    title = GOT_Story_Country.441.t 
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.441.d 
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country441 
 
    trigger = { 
        tag = DRA
		date > 1940.10.10
        date < 1940.10.11
		has_war_with = BOL
		BOL = { has_capitulated = no }
		has_country_leader = {
			name = "Stannis Baratheon"
			ruling_only = yes
		}
		BOL = { controls_state = 156 } 
		has_global_flag = GOT_Stannis_Aids_Wall_Flag
    } 
 
    fire_only_once = yes 
 
    option = { 
        name = GOT_Story_Country.441.a 
 
        ai_chance = { 
            factor = 10 
        } 
 
        add_stability = -0.10
        add_war_support = -0.10
        army_experience = 100
		
		remove_country_leader_role = {
			character = DRA_Shireen_Baratheon
			ideology = DR_subideology
		}
		
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = DRA_Davos_Seaworth
			country_leader = {
				expire = "1965.1.1"
				ideology = DR_subideology
				traits = { 
					stout_defender 
					#humble 
					hawk 
					popular_figurehead 
					reluctant_veteran 
					political_dancer 
					#voice_of_restraint
				}
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
 
        every_other_country = { 
            limit = { 
                exists = yes 
            } 
            news_event = { 
                id = GOT_Story_News.441 
                hours = 6 
            } 
        } 
    } 
}

    id = GOT_Story_Country.446 
    title = GOT_Story_Country.446.t 
    desc = GOT_Story_Country.446.d 
    picture = GFX_report_event_GOT_Story_Country446 
 
    trigger = { 
        tag = NWA
		date > 1940.11.15
        date < 1940.12.15
		is_in_faction_with = WIL
		has_country_leader = {
			name = "Jon Snow"
			ruling_only = yes
		}
    } 
 
    fire_only_once = yes

        name = GOT_Story_Country.446.a 
 
        ai_chance = { 
            factor = 60 
        }

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_government = communism }
			}
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = communism
			}
		}
		add_popularity = {
			popularity = 0.50
			ideology = communism
		}
		
		start_civil_war = {
			ideology = fascism
			size = 0.40
		}
 
        every_other_country = { 
            limit = { 
                exists = yes 
            } 
            news_event = { 
                id = GOT_Story_News.446 
                hours = 6 
            } 
        } 
    } 
	
	option = { 
        name = GOT_Story_Country.446.a 
 
        ai_chance = { 
            factor = 40 
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
        } 
 
        add_stability = -0.05 
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_government = fascism }
			}
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = fascism
			}
		}
		add_popularity = {
			popularity = 0.50
			ideology = fascism
		}

			ideology = communism
			size = 0.40
		}
